- **strategist**: Clean-context generator of the three strategic approaches for an SDD feature idea. Use it in the ideation pass of specify (hard depth). Thus the spec selects an approach from real options, not the first idea. Read-only. It returns three approaches, each with Name · Thesis · For-whom · Outcome-metric · Key-trade-off · Effort-signal.
